    • 引入编码函数并分析其凸性随D变化。
    • 推广了计数估计、格曲线几何、唯一性及McShane-Hines恒等式。
    • 表明经典D=0情形是更一般图景的一部分。
    💡 研究一般D的Markov型方程解树的结构，推广了经典D=0情形，并给出编码函数的凸性等性质。
    📖 中文摘要

    正整数解x^2+y^2+z^2-xyz=D对应于重要的Markov数当D=0时。从给定的解三元组出发，通过Vieta对合可找到另外三个解，构成无限的解树。若从三个大于2的实数出发，则得到穿孔环面上闭测地线的长度。本文研究任意实数D的这些树结构。一个最初与同调上的范数相关的连续函数编码了每棵树上的所有数。本文一般性地发展了该函数的性质，给出了自包含的阐述，表明通常的D=0情形是更大图景的一部分。编码函数图在D<4时是凸的，D=4时是直线，D>4时是凹的。其他推论包括对所有D推广了：这些树上计数数的估计、相应格曲线的几何描述、唯一性条件以及McShane和Hines的恒等式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Positive integer solutions to $x^2+y^2+z^2- x y z =D$ correspond to the important Markov (Markoff) numbers when $D=0$. From a given solution triple, three more are found with Vieta involutions, making an infinite tree of solutions. Starting instead with three real numbers greater than $2$ gives lengths of closed geodesics in a punctured torus. In this paper we study these tree structures for any real $D$. A continuous function, originally related to a norm on homology, encodes all the numbers on each of these trees. The properties of this function are developed here in general, with a self-contained exposition, showing that the usual $D=0$ case is part of a bigger picture. Encoding function graphs are shown to be convex for $D<4$, straight lines for $D=4$ and concave for $D>4$. Among other consequences are generalizations to all $D$ of: estimates for counting numbers on these trees, descriptions of the geometry of the corresponding lattice curves, uniqueness conditions, and identities of McShane and Hines.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14149

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究高维随机格薄壳中所有点的球面统计。格点之间的精确关系使得仅基于球面几何的预测何时成立尚不清楚。我们针对若干统计量回答了这个问题，包括壳点数、其方向的平衡性以及它们之间差值的出现、重复和分布。我们识别出当壳半径增长时的尖锐阈值，并证明这些统计量经历若干不同的相变。一个壳可能已经与一个几何预测一致，但仍与另一个强烈不同。我们的结果对单个采样格成立，概率随维数增长趋于1。它们包括在整个壳上成立的估计、接近相变阈值的界，以及对随机平移格的扩展。Lean形式化验证了主要结果，假设代码中陈述的经典公式和概率模型。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the spherical statistics of all the points in a thin shell of a high-dimensional random lattice. The exact relations between lattice points make it unclear when predictions based only on spherical geometry should hold. We answer this question for several statistics, including the number of shell points, the balance of their directions, and the occurrence, repetition, and distribution of differences between them. We identify sharp thresholds as the shell radius grows and show that these statistics undergo several distinct phase transitions. A shell can already agree with one geometric prediction while still differing strongly from another. Our results hold for a single sampled lattice, with probability tending to one as the dimension grows. They include estimates that hold across a complete shell, bounds close to the transition thresholds, and extensions to randomly shifted lattices.   8. [2609.13626] 沿中间增长序列的点态遍历定理
    Pointwise ergodic theorems along sequences of intermediate growth
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究沿中间增长序列的遍历平均点态收敛问题。
    • 首次证明 $\lfloor\exp((\log n)^c)\rfloor$ 对 $c\in(1,8/7)$ 是普遍 $L^p$-好的。
    • 方法结合单频圆法与 Vinogradov 指数和方法。
    • 肯定回答 1980 年代公开问题，推进 Bellow 纲领。
    💡 首次证明沿显式中间增长序列的遍历平均点态收敛，证明 $\lfloor\exp((\log n)^c)\rfloor$ 对 $c\in(1,8/7)$ 是普遍 $L^p$-好的。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们建立了沿显式确定性中间增长序列（即增长快于任何多项式但慢于任何指数）的遍历平均的第一个点态收敛结果。特别地，我们证明序列 $(\lfloor \exp((\log n)^c)\rfloor)_{n\in\mathbb{Z}+}$，其中 $c\in(1,8/7)$，对每个 $p\in(1,\infty]$ 是普遍 $L^p$-好的。这肯定地回答了可追溯到 1980 年代中期的公开问题，并推进了 Bellow 在同一十年早期发起的关于点态遍历定理中 $L^p$-好序列刻画的研究纲领。证明结合了所谓单频圆法和对 Vinogradov 方法的精细应用，用于估计相位涉及 $(\lfloor \exp((\log n)^c)\rfloor)_{n\in\mathbb{Z}_+}$ 的指数和。我们分析的一个有趣特征，类似于 Riemann zeta 函数无零点区域研究中出现的估计，是论证依赖于经典 Vinogradov 方法，即需要对 Vinogradov 丢番图方程组的解数进行显式依赖于系统参数的估计。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We establish the first pointwise convergence result for ergodic averages with iterates along explicit and deterministic sequences of intermediate growth, that is, growing faster than any polynomial but slower than any exponential. In particular, we show that the sequence $(\lfloor \exp((\log n)^c)\rfloor)_{n\in\mathbb{Z}+}$, with $c\in(1,8/7)$, is universally $L^p$-good for every $p\in(1,\infty]$. This gives an affirmative answer to an open problem dating back to the mid 1980s and contributes to Bellow's program, initiated in the earlier part of the same decade, on the characterization of $L^p$-good sequences in pointwise ergodic theorems. The proof combines the so-called one-frequency circle method with a delicate application of Vinogradov's method for estimating exponential sums whose phases involve $\big(\lfloor \exp((\log n)^c)\rfloor\big)_{n\in\mathbb{Z}_+}$. An interesting feature of our analysis, reminiscent of estimates arising in the study of the zero-free region of the Riemann zeta function, is that the argument relies on the classical Vinogradov method, in the sense that it necessitates estimates on the number of solutions for the Vinogradov system of Diophantine equations with explicit dependence on the system's parameters.

  10. [2609.13852] 素与非素互素数：精确构造与精确阈值
    Prime and Nonprime Totatives: A Sharp Construction and Exact Thresholds
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 $n$ 的素互素数与合互素数个数之间的比较。
    • 构造显式合互素数族，证明其密度达到最优常数 $e^{-\gamma}$。
    • 确定 $\phi(n)>k\pi(n)$ 与 $B(n)>kA(n)$ 的精确阈值及关系。
    • 结果深化了对互素数分布与最小阶问题的理解。
    💡 构造显式复合互素数族，证明其密度达到最优常数 $e^{-\gamma}$，并确定 $\phi(n)>k\pi(n)$ 与 $B(n)>kA(n)$ 的精确阈值。
    📖 中文摘要

    对 $n\ge2$，设 $A(n)=\pi(n)-\omega(n)$ 和 $B(n)=\phi(n)-\pi(n)+\omega(n)$ 分别表示 $n$ 的素互素数与合互素数个数，其中 $1$ 计入 $B(n)$。我们通过将适当限制的素互素数无平方乘积与一个更大的素互素数（唯一最大素因子）补全，构造了一个显式单射参数化的合互素数族 $\mathcal C_n$，使得参数化可恢复。我们证明 $\lvert\mathcal C_n\rvert/A(n)\ge (e^{-\gamma}-o(1))\log A(n)/\log\log A(n)$，实现了尖锐的经典主常数 $e^{-\gamma}$。对同一族，用相同定义参数，我们得到误差为 $(\log\log A(n))^{-1/4}$ 阶的有效改进，且常数绝对可计算。经典最小阶结果与素数定理表明 $e^{-\gamma}$ 是最优的：没有合互素数内的族能以更大主常数满足该尺度的统一下界。我们还确定了精确的最终阈值。设 $N_k$ 和 $M_k$ 分别为使 $\phi(n)>k\pi(n)$ 对所有 $n\ge N_k$ 成立和 $B(n)>kA(n)$ 对所有 $n\ge M_k$ 成立的最小整数。我们证明对每个 $k\ge1$ 有 $M_k\le N_{k+1}$，精确确定 $N_1,\ldots,N_6$ 和 $M_1,\ldots,M_5$，并得到 $1\le k\le5$ 时 $M_k=N_{k+1}$。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For \(n\ge2\), let \(A(n)=\pi(n)-\omega(n)\) and \(B(n)=\phi(n)-\pi(n)+\omega(n)\) denote the numbers of prime and nonprime totatives of \(n\), respectively, with \(1\) included in \(B(n)\). We construct an explicit injectively parametrized family \(\mathcal C_n\) of composite totatives by completing suitably restricted squarefree products of prime totatives with a larger prime totative that is the unique largest prime factor, making the parametrization recoverable. We prove \(\lvert\mathcal C_n\rvert/A(n)\ge (e^{-\gamma}-o(1))\log A(n)/\log\log A(n)\), realizing the sharp classical leading constant \(e^{-\gamma}\). For the same family, with the same defining parameters, we obtain an effective refinement with error of order \((\log\log A(n))^{-1/4}\) and absolute effectively computable constants. Classical minimal-order results and the prime number theorem show that \(e^{-\gamma}\) is optimal: no family contained in the nonprime totatives can satisfy a uniform lower bound at this scale with a larger leading constant. We also determine exact eventual thresholds. Let \(N_k\) and \(M_k\) be the least integers such that \(\phi(n)>k\pi(n)\) for every \(n\ge N_k\) and \(B(n)>kA(n)\) for every \(n\ge M_k\), respectively. We prove \(M_k\le N_{k+1}\) for every \(k\ge1\), determine \(N_1,\ldots,N_6\) and \(M_1,\ldots,M_5\) exactly, and obtain \(M_k=N_{k+1}\) for \(1\le k\le5\).

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13852

This improves Wooley's bound, and we show that the result is best possible. Our approach combines Hooley's weighted representation function for sums of two squares with Green's $W$-trick. We also give a necessary and sufficient condition, in terms of covering systems, for almost all odd integers to be represented as the sum of a squarefree number and a power of two, answering a question of Granville and Soundararajan.

    📖 中文摘要

    设 $b, p\in {\mathbb Z}$，$b\ge 2$，$p\ge 3$ 为素数。若 $b^{p-1}\equiv 1 \pmod{p^2}$，则称 $p$ 为基 $b$ 的广义 Wieferich 素数。当 $b=2$ 时，$p$ 也称为 Wieferich 素数。称首一多项式 $f(x)\in {\mathbb Z}[x]$ 是单生成的，若 $f(x)$ 在 ${\mathbb Q}$ 上不可约且 $\{1,\theta,\theta^2,\ldots,\theta^{\deg(f)-1}\}$ 是 ${\mathbb Q}(\theta)$ 的整数环的基，其中 $f(\theta)=0$。最近，三项式 $x^{2n}+bx^n+b$ 的单生成性的充要条件已被给出，其中包括基 $b$ 的 Wieferich 素数同余以及涉及某 Lucas 序列的同余。本文中，我们对另一类三项式证明类似结果，且不依赖于任何涉及 Lucas 序列的同余条件。此外，我们将该结果推广到相关的 $N$ 项式类，对任意 $N\ge 4$。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $b, p\in {\mathbb Z}$ with $b\ge 2$ and $p\ge 3$ a prime. If $b^{p-1}\equiv 1 \pmod{p^2}$, then $p$ is called a {\em generalized Wieferich prime base $b$}, or more succinctly, a {\em base-$b$ Wieferich prime}. When $b=2$, $p$ is also known simply as a Wieferich prime. We say that a monic polynomial $f(x)\in {\mathbb Z}[x]$ is {\em monogenic} if $f(x)$ is irreducible over ${\mathbb Q}$ and $\{1,\theta,\theta^2,\ldots,\theta^{\deg(f)-1}\}$ is a basis for the ring of integers of ${\mathbb Q}(\theta)$, where $f(\theta)=0$. Recently, necessary and sufficient conditions for the monogenicity of the trinomials $x^{2n}+bx^n+b$ were given that included base-$b$ Wieferich prime congruences, and also congruences involving a certain Lucas sequence. In this article, we prove a similar result for a different class of trinomials that does not rely on any congruence condition involving a Lucas sequence. Furthermore, we extend this result to a related class of $N$-nomials, for any $N\ge 4$.

  13. [2609.13932] 分圆类群某些特征空间消失的密度
    Density of Vanishing of Certain Eigenspaces of Cyclotomic Class Groups
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究分圆域类群特征空间在特定素数密度下的消失与秩界。
    • 证明对相对密度一的素数，奇块阶有界、偶块秩有界。
    • 对 $d=4,6$ 证明块消失，验证 Vandiver 猜想；对 $d=8,10,12$ 验证循环性猜想。
    • 方法结合 Dirichlet L-值极限律、Bernoulli 范数整性与反射。
    💡 证明对相对密度一的素数，分圆类群特定特征空间块消失或秩有界，验证 Vandiver 与 Iwasawa 猜想的部分情形。
    📖 中文摘要

    对奇素数 $p$，设 $A_j$ 为 $\mathbb Q(\zeta_p)$ 的 $p$-primary 类群的 $\omega^j$-特征空间。固定偶整数 $d\ge4$，令 $N=(p-1)/d$，$U_d=(\mathbb Z/d\mathbb Z)^\times$。对相对密度一的素数 $p\equiv d+1\pmod{2d}$，我们证明奇块 $\bigoplus_{a\in U_d}A_{aN}$ 的阶至多为 $p^{\varphi(d)/2-1}$，反射表明偶块 $\bigoplus_{a\in U_d}A_{p-aN}$ 的 $p$-秩至多为 $\varphi(d)/2-1$。对每个 $d\in\{4,6\}$，两个块对相对密度一的素数 $p\equiv d+1\pmod{2d}$ 消失；特别地，偶分量按 Vandiver 猜想消失。对每个 $d\in\{8,10,12\}$，奇块对同一等差数列中相对密度一的素数阶至多为 $p$，故每个直和项 $A_{aN}$，$a\in U_d$，是循环的，符合 Iwasawa 循环性猜想。证明使用 Dirichlet $L$-值乘积的无原子极限律、广义 Bernoulli 范数的整性、相对类数公式和反射。另有一个独立精确计算证明对每个奇素数 $p$ 有 $A_{34}=0$；一个单文件 PARI/GP 程序可复现该计算。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For an odd prime $p$, let $A_j$ be the $\omega^j$-eigenspace of the $p$-primary class group of $\mathbb Q(\zeta_p)$. Fix an even integer $d\ge4$, put $N=(p-1)/d$, and let $U_d=(\mathbb Z/d\mathbb Z)^\times$. For a relative density-one set of primes $p\equiv d+1\pmod{2d}$, we prove that the odd block $\bigoplus_{a\in U_d}A_{aN}$ has order at most $p^{\varphi(d)/2-1}$, and reflection shows that the even block $\bigoplus_{a\in U_d}A_{p-aN}$ has $p$-rank at most $\varphi(d)/2-1$. For each $d\in\{4,6\}$, both blocks vanish for a relative density-one set of primes $p\equiv d+1\pmod{2d}$; in particular, the even components vanish in accordance with Vandiver's conjecture. For each $d\in\{8,10,12\}$, the odd block has order at most $p$ for a relative density-one set of primes in the same progression, so every summand $A_{aN}$, $a\in U_d$, is cyclic, in accordance with Iwasawa's cyclicity conjecture. The proof uses an atomless limiting law for products of Dirichlet $L$-values, integrality of generalized Bernoulli norms, the relative class-number formula and reflection. A separate exact computation proves $A_{34}=0$ for every odd prime $p$; a single-file PARI/GP program reproduces the calculation.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们给出非阿基米德局部域上局部 epsilon 因子的 Langlands 第二主引理的局部证明，涵盖混合特征与等特征情形。该引理比较双循环扩张中两个中间域的特征的局部常数。它是 Langlands 构造局部 Weil 表示 epsilon 因子时所用的恒等式之一。证明建立在 Dwork、Langlands 和 Lakkis 的工作之上。Langlands 将第二主引理归功于 Dwork，但 Dwork 未发表完整证明。Lakkis 给出了第二主引理的详细局部处理。对于奇素数次数，他的论证证明了所需等式。然而在二次情形，它只证明等式在相差一个符号的意义下成立。本文的新点在于野二进情形，即剩余特征为二的非阿基米德局部域上的双二次扩张。对于双二次扩张，第一主引理确定了所需等式的平方，留下可能的符号。我们通过比较 Lamprecht 公式中出现的有限和来确定该符号。这完成了第二主引理的证明，包括等特征情形。OpenAI ChatGPT 在证明发展中被广泛使用。论证的很大一部分由长局部计算组成，原则上可用标准方法完成，但需要大量时间。因此使用 ChatGPT 加速技术工作：检查计算并与 Dwork、Langlands 和 Lakkis 的论证比较，检测中间版本证明中的不一致。作者检查了最终数学论证并对结果负责。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We give a local proof of Langlands's Second Main Lemma for local epsilon factors over nonarchimedean local fields, in mixed and equal characteristic. The lemma compares local constants of characters of two intermediate fields in a bicyclic extension. It is one of the identities used in Langlands's construction of epsilon factors of local Weil representations. The proof builds on the work of Dwork, Langlands, and Lakkis. Langlands attributes the Second Main Lemma to Dwork, but Dwork did not publish a complete proof. Lakkis gave a detailed local treatment of the Second Main Lemma. For odd prime degree, his argument proves the required equality. In degree two, however, it proves only that the equality holds up to sign. The new point in the present paper is the wild dyadic case, i.e. biquadratic extensions over a nonarchimedean local field of residue characteristic two. For a biquadratic extension the First Main Lemma determines the square of the required equality, leaving a possible sign. We determine this sign by comparing the finite sums occurring in Lamprecht's formula. This completes the proof of the Second Main Lemma, including the equal-characteristic case. OpenAI ChatGPT was used extensively in the development of this proof. A substantial part of the argument consists of long local calculations, which are in principle accessible by standard methods, but would have required a very large amount of time. ChatGPT was therefore used to accelerate this technical work: to check calculations and compare them with the arguments of Dwork, Langlands, and Lakkis, and detect inconsistencies in intermediate versions of the proof. The author checked the final mathematical arguments and assumes responsibility for the results.

    🤖 AI 总结

    • 通过双高度计数估计和自适应行列式论证，证明此类函数必为有理函数。
    • 否定回答了 Mahler 问题，并给出定量局部结果：非有理解析函数下 Liouville 数的像有无理性指数上界。
    • 方法结合 Wronskian 次水平估计与 Farey 分离。
    💡 否定解决 Mahler 问题：不存在将 Liouville 数映为 Liouville 数的超越整函数，并给出定量局部结果。
    📖 中文摘要

    Maillet 的经典定理指出，每个非常数有理函数将 Liouville 数映为 Liouville 数。1984 年，Mahler 问是否存在具有相同性质的超越整函数。我们否定地回答了这个问题。更一般地，我们证明区间上具有该性质的每个实解析函数都是 $\mathbb{R}(x)$ 中有理函数的限制。底层的局部结果是定量且一致的：存在绝对常数，使得对每个非有理实解析函数 $f$，其定义域中每个非空开子区间都包含一个 Liouville 数，其像在 $f$ 下的无理性指数被该常数界定。主要工具是对非有理解析图的有理逼近的双高度计数估计，其中源分母和目标分母独立处理。其证明结合了自适应行列式论证、一致 Wronskian 次水平估计和 Farey 分离；嵌套区间构造则给出局部定量结果。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A classical theorem of Maillet states that every nonconstant rational function with rational coefficients maps Liouville numbers to Liouville numbers. In 1984, Mahler asked whether there exists a transcendental entire function with the same property. We resolve this question in the negative. More generally, we prove that every real-analytic function on an interval with this property is the restriction of a rational function in $\mathbb{R}(x)$. The underlying local result is quantitative and uniform: there exists an absolute constant such that, for every nonrational real-analytic function $f$, every nonempty open subinterval of its domain contains a Liouville number whose image under $f$ has irrationality exponent bounded by that constant. The main ingredient is a two-height counting estimate for rational approximation to nonrational analytic graphs, with the source and target denominators treated independently. Its proof combines an adaptive determinant argument with uniform Wronskian sublevel estimates and Farey separation; a nested-interval construction then yields the local quantitative result.

  23. [2609.14265] 有限秩上限 Enots-Wolley 序列的满射性
    Surjectivity of Finite Rank-Capped Enots-Wolley Sequences
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究有限秩上限的 Enots-Wolley 序列的满射性。
    • 证明每个满足素数因子数限制的整数都会出现在序列中。
    • 通过反证法，利用 Landau 估计比较真值与完整总体的对数阶，导出矛盾。
    • 结果不适用于无限制的 Enots-Wolley 序列。
    💡 证明对每个固定秩上限 $K\ge2$，满足素数因子数限制的 Enots-Wolley 序列包含所有允许的整数。
    📖 中文摘要

    对每个固定整数 $K\ge2$，我们考虑 Enots-Wolley 序列，其中初始项 $1,2$ 之后的每一项要求有 2 到 $K$ 个不同素因子。我们证明满足该限制的每个整数都出现。若某个精确素支撑 $T$ 仅被有限次选择，则在有限截断后，满足 $T$ 的项形成短片段，且每个完整项都迫使较早的真项在可比较的数值高度出现。$|T|=K$ 的情形被直接排除。在剩余情形 $|T|<K$ 中，在记录真值 $H$ 处，贪心性迫使所有低于 $H$ 的、包含 $T$ 中恰好一个素数的未阻塞秩 $K$ 整数都已出现。固定阶 Landau 估计表明该真值总体具有阶 $H(\log\log H)^{K-2}/\log H$，而同一尺度上所有可能的完整总体具有严格更小的对数阶。此矛盾证明满射性。该定理针对每个固定秩上限，未解决无限制 Enots-Wolley 序列的满射性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For each fixed integer $K\ge2$, we consider the Enots--Wolley sequence in which every term after the initial $1,2$ is required to have between two and $K$ distinct prime divisors. We prove that every integer satisfying this restriction occurs. If an exact prime support $T$ were selected only finitely often, then after a finite cutoff the terms meeting $T$ would form short episodes, and every full term would force an earlier proper term at comparable numerical height. The case $|T|=K$ is then ruled out directly. In the remaining case $|T|<K$, at a record proper value $H$, greediness forces every unblocked rank-$K$ integer below $H$ containing exactly one prime of $T$ to have occurred earlier. Fixed-order Landau estimates show that this proper population has order $H(\log\log H)^{K-2}/\log H$, while the entire possible full population on the same scale has strictly smaller logarithmic order. This contradiction proves surjectivity. The theorem concerns each fixed rank cap and does not settle surjectivity of the unrestricted Enots--Wolley sequence.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14265

    📖 中文摘要

    设 $p$ 为奇素数，记 $A\dotplus B=\{a+b:a\in A,\ b\in B,\ a\ne b\}$。我们分类所有满足 $|A|+|B|=p$、$|A|>|B|=\ell\ge1$ 且 $|A\dotplus B|=p-2$ 的集合对 $A,B\subseteq\mathbb{F}_p$。将两个缺失和规范化为 $\{0,1\}$ 后，我们得到恰好 $\ell+1$ 个显式模型。分类迫使 $B\subseteq A$，并对固定 $p,\ell$ 给出恰好 $\binom p2(\ell+1)$ 个有序对和 $1+\lfloor\ell/2\rfloor$ 个在同时仿射变换下的等价类。我们确定何时任一集合为等差数列，并计算精确的差集基数。对 $\ell\ge3$ 和 $p\ge4\ell-5$，基数 $|B-B|$ 在固定 $p,\ell$ 下决定等价类。证明使用穿孔平移和循环分量计数。我们还展示 $\mathbb{F}_{13}$ 中一个临界对，大小差为三且 $|A|+|B|=p-2$，它否定了边界下方的一个拟议逆命题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $p$ be an odd prime, and write $A\RS B=\{a+b:a\in A,\ b\in B,\ a\ne b\}$. We classify all pairs $A,B\subseteq\Fp$ satisfying $|A|+|B|=p$, $|A|>|B|=\ell\ge1$, and $|A\RS B|=p-2$. After normalizing the two missing sums to $\{0,1\}$, we obtain exactly $\ell+1$ explicit models. The classification forces $B\subseteq A$ and yields, for fixed $p,\ell$, exactly $\binom p2(\ell+1)$ ordered pairs and $1+\lfloor\ell/2\rfloor$ equivalence classes under simultaneous affine transformations. We determine when either set is an arithmetic progression and compute exact difference-set cardinalities. For $\ell\ge3$ and $p\ge4\ell-5$, the cardinality $|B-B|$ determines the equivalence class at fixed $p,\ell$. The proof uses punctured translates and cyclic component counting. We also exhibit a critical pair in $\mathbb F_{13}$, with size gap three and $|A|+|B|=p-2$, that contradicts a proposed inverse statement below the boundary.

  35. [2609.14893] Sylvester 猜想的证明
    A proof of Sylvester's conjecture
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 证明 Sylvester 猜想：$p\equiv4,7,8\pmod9$ 的素数可表为两个有理立方数之和。
    • 对剩余类 $p\equiv8\pmod9$，证明椭圆曲线 $E_p$ 的解析秩为一。
    • 方法改编 Rankin-Selberg 构造，利用 3-同源下降和 Heegner 点非挠性。
    • 关键创新是在取迹前取 $\lambda$-除，分析 Frobenius 证明除边界非零。
    💡 证明 Sylvester 猜想：每个 $p\equiv4,7,8\pmod9$ 的素数可表为两个有理立方数之和。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明 Sylvester 猜想，该猜想源于他 1879 年对三元三次方程的研究，即每个素数 $p\equiv4,7,8\pmod9$ 是两个有理立方数之和。Elkies 在 1994 年宣布了对类 $4$ 和 $7$ 的证明，Yin 最近提供了完整证明。对于剩余的类 $p\equiv8\pmod9$，我们证明椭圆曲线 $E_p:y^2=x^3+p^2/4$ 的解析秩为一，正如 Birch 和 Swinnerton-Dyer 猜想所预测，因此 $p$ 是两个有理立方数之和。证明首先改编自作者关于 CM 椭圆曲线秩一逆定理的工作中的辅助 Rankin-Selberg 构造。所得卷积分解为 $E_p$ 的 $L$-函数和一个互补 $L$-函数。Chan 的 3-同源下降和秩零逆定理表明互补中心 $L$-值非零，因此只需证明相关 Heegner 点的三次分量非挠。一个基本困难是底层 CM 轨道的未加权 Hecke 迹消失。我们的决定性想法是在取迹之前先取 $\lambda$-除，其中 $\lambda=1-\omega$，$\omega$ 为本原三次单位根。我们通过分析 $p$ 处的 Frobenius 证明所得除边界非零。CM 轨道上的 Galois 作用和分歧理论然后将此非零性转移到三次分量。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove Sylvester's conjecture, originating in his 1879 study of ternary cubic equations, that every prime $p\equiv4,7,8\pmod9$ is a sum of two rational cubes. Elkies announced a proof for the classes $4$ and $7$ in 1994, and Yin recently supplied a complete proof. For the remaining class $p\equiv8\pmod9$, we prove that the elliptic curve $E_p:y^2=x^3+p^2/4$ has analytic rank one, as predicted by the Birch and Swinnerton-Dyer conjecture, and so $p$ is a sum of two rational cubes. The proof begins by adapting the auxiliary Rankin--Selberg construction from the authors' work on the rank one converse for CM elliptic curves. The resulting convolution factors as the $L$-function of $E_p$ times a complementary $L$-function. Chan's $3$-isogeny descent and the rank zero converse show that the complementary central $L$-value is non-zero, and so it suffices to prove that a cubic component of the associated Heegner point is non-torsion. A basic difficulty is that the unweighted Hecke trace of the underlying CM orbit vanishes. Our decisive idea is to take $\lambda$-division before taking the trace, where $\lambda=1-\omega$ and $\omega$ is a primitive cube root of unity. We prove that the resulting division boundary is non-zero by analysing Frobenius at $p$. The Galois action on the CM orbit and ramification theory then transfer this non-vanishing to the cubic component.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14893
  36. [2609.14915] Hecke 特征值和的一般大小为 $o(\sqrt{x})$
    The typical size of Hecke eigenvalue sums is $o(\sqrt{x})$
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 Hecke 特征值和 $\sum_{n\le x}\lambda_f(n)$ 的典型大小。
    • 证明加权二阶矩上界，在 $x\le k^{0.99}$ 时匹配猜想，并得到 $o(\sqrt{x})$ 结果。
    • 方法使用 Cogdell-Michel 概率模型，确定低阶矩的阶。
    • 结果支持 Hecke 特征值和在典型意义下小于 $\sqrt{x}$。
    💡 证明 Hecke 特征值和在典型意义下为 $o(\sqrt{x})$，匹配猜想上界。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 $\mathcal{H}_k$ 为 $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ 上权为 $k$ 的归一化全纯 Hecke 尖点形式集合。对每个 $f\in\mathcal{H}_k$，设 $\lambda_f(n)$ 为归一化 Hecke 算子 $T_n$ 的相应特征值。我们证明 \[\sum_{f \in \mathcal{H}_k}\omega_f \left|\sum_{n \leq x} \lambda_f(n)\right|^{2q}\] 的非平凡上界，其中 $k$ 为正偶数，$1\leqslant x\leqslant k$，$0\leqslant q \leqslant 1$，$\omega_f = \frac{\Gamma(k-1)} {(4\pi)^{k-1}\langle f,f\rangle} = \frac{2\pi^2} {(k-1)L(1,\mathrm{Sym}^2 f)}$。我们的估计在 $1\leqslant x\leqslant k^{0.99}$ 时匹配猜想上界。特别地，当 $x$ 和 $k/x$ 都随 $k$ 趋于无穷时，得到 \[\sum_{f \in \mathcal{H}_k}\omega_f \left|\sum_{n \leq x} \lambda_f(n)\right|=o(\sqrt{x}).\] 我们还证明与 $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ 的 Hecke-Maass 尖点形式相关的 Hecke 特征值和低阶矩的上界。我们通过 Cogdell-Michel 提出的概率模型研究这些和，并确定该模型低阶矩的阶，这等价于确定极限中 Hecke 特征值和低阶矩的阶。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\mathcal{H}_k$ denote the set of normalized holomorphic Hecke cusp forms of weight $k$ for the full modular group $ \mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb Z)$. For each $f\in\mathcal{H}_k$, let $\lambda_f(n)$ denote the corresponding eigenvalue of the normalized Hecke operator $T_n$. We prove nontrivial upper bounds for \[\sum_{f \in \mathcal{H}_k}\omega_f \left|\sum_{n \leq x} \lambda_f(n)\right|^{2q},\] where $k$ is a positive even integer, $1\leqslant x\leqslant k$, $0\leqslant q \leqslant 1$ and $ \omega_f = \frac{\Gamma(k-1)} {(4\pi)^{k-1}\langle f,f\rangle} = \frac{2\pi^2} {(k-1)L(1,\mathrm{Sym}^2 f)}. $ Our estimates match the conjecturally sharp upper bound for $1\leqslant x\leqslant k^{0.99}$. In particular, whenever both $x$ and $k/x$ tend to infinity with $k$, we obtain \[\sum_{f \in \mathcal{H}_k}\omega_f \left|\sum_{n \leq x} \lambda_f(n)\right|=o(\sqrt{x}).\] We also prove upper bounds for low moments of sums of Hecke eigenvalues associated with Hecke--Maass cusp forms for $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb Z)$. We study these sums through the probabilistic model proposed by Cogdell--Michel. We also determine the order of magnitude of low moments of this probabilistic model, which is equivalent to determining the order of magnitude of low moments of Hecke eigenvalue sums in the limit.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14915

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the arithmetic constraints that two-term Rogers dilogarithm relations impose on rank-two Nahm systems with mixed denominator steps. Monomial complement equations give a uniform conversion to quadratic data in every algebraic degree. Within a specified ten-record simplest-cubic family, positive coupling, positive definiteness, and integer-valued exponents leave, for m > 2, exactly the steps (1,3), (1,13), and (1,31). At the smallest compatible modulus 31, the required support cannot be a multiplicative subgroup coset. Quadratic examples include known product identities and two systems for which the first radial correction excludes every individual modular sum with rational linear terms. For the index-13 system, we prove a two-term Rogers identity and recover its arguments from the matrix of a previously proved two-dimensional Fricke transformation. Its cusp growth agrees with the resulting saddle action, and an independent calculation reproduces all three leading Fricke amplitudes exactly. The associated two-summand product evaluations remain conjectural; the small rank and dimension contrast with the classical rank-five, six-component Andrews-Gordon construction at the same modulus. A mixed-signature cubic family illustrates the limits of the arithmetic restriction

    🤖 AI 总结

    • 证明渐近几乎所有 $p$-秩零 EO 分层都与超奇异轨迹相交。
    • 应用于酉 PEL Shimura 簇，给出相交 EO 分层数目的下界。
    💡 给出 EO 分层与超奇异轨迹相交的显式组合判据，证明几乎所有 $p$-秩零 EO 分层都相交。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们给出一个显式组合判据，用于判断特征 $p>2$ 的 $g$ 维主极化阿贝尔簇模叠中的 Ekedahl-Oort（EO）分层是否与超奇异轨迹相交。该判据通过将基本仿射 Deligne-Lusztig 簇的非空性判据转化为对索引 EO 分层的初等序列的显式条件而得到。此外，我们给出与超奇异轨迹不相交的 $p$-秩零 EO 分层数目的显式界。由此证明渐近几乎每个 $p$-秩零 EO 分层都与超奇异轨迹相交。作为应用，我们还给出 signature $(a,b)$ 的酉 PEL Shimura 簇上与超奇异轨迹相交的 EO 分层数目的下界。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We give an explicit combinatorial criterion for an Ekedahl-Oort (EO) stratum in the moduli stack of principally polarized abelian varieties of dimension $g$ in characteristic $p > 2$ to meet the supersingular locus. The criterion is obtained by translating a non-emptiness criterion for basic affine Deligne-Lusztig varieties, into an explicit condition on the elementary sequences indexing the EO strata. Furthermore, we give explicit bounds for the number of EO strata of $p$-rank zero which are disjoint from the supersingular locus. As a result, we prove that asymptotically almost every EO stratum of $p$-rank zero meets the supersingular locus. As an application, we also give a lower bound for the number of EO strata meeting the supersingular locus on unitary PEL Shimura varieties of signature $(a,b)$.

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究连续整数和连续素数移位后小于y的最大素因子的比较问题。
    • 方法基于解析数论中的筛法和渐近估计。

    • 这些结果改进了Rivat和Wang的先前工作。
    💡 研究连续整数和连续素数移位后小于y的最大素因子的大小比较，改进Rivat和Wang的结果。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于整数n>1，令P^+(n)为n的最大素因子，P_y^+(n)表示n的不超过y的最大素因子。Erdős和Turán的一个猜想断言满足P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)的整数的渐近密度为1/2。此外，Wang猜想当x→∞时，#{p≤x: P^+(p-1)<P^+(p+1)} ~ π(x)/2。本文中，我们证明以下结果。对于任意3≤y≪x^{o(1)}，当x→∞时，有#{n≤x: P_y^+(n)<P_y^+(n+1)} ~ x/2，#{p≤x: P_y^+(p-1)<P_y^+(p+1)} ~ π(x)/2。对于任意0<α<17/32，令y=x^α，则存在h(α)>0使得#{p≤x: P_y^+(p-1)<P_y^+(p+1)} ≥ (h(α)+o(1))π(x)。特别地，函数h满足lim_{α→0+} h(α)=1/2。类似结果对#{n≤x: P_y^+(n)<P_y^+(n+1)}也成立。这些改进了Rivat (2001)和Wang (2019)的结果。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For an integer $n > 1$, let $P^+(n)$ be the largest prime factor of $n$, and let $P_y^+(n)$ denote the largest prime factor of $n$ not exceeding $y$. One of Erd\H{o}s and Tur\'an's conjectures asserts that the asymptotic density of integers $n$ satisfying $P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)$ is $1/2$. Furthermore, Wang conjectures that for $x\rightarrow\infty$, one has $\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)<P^+(p+1)\}\sim \pi(x)/2$. In this paper, we show the following results. For any $3\leq y\ll x^{o(1)}$, for $x\rightarrow\infty$, we have \begin{align*} &\#\{n\leq x:P^+_y(n)<P^+_y(n+1)\}\sim \frac{1}{2}x, \\&\#\{p\leq x:P^+_y(p-1)<P^+_y(p+1)\}\sim \frac{1}{2}\pi(x). \end{align*} For any $0<\alpha<17/32$, let $y=x^\alpha$. Then there exists $h(\alpha)>0$ such that \begin{align*} \#\{p\leq x:P_y^+(p-1)<P_y^+(p+1)\}\geq(h(\alpha)+o(1))\pi(x). \end{align*} In particular, the function $h$ satisfies $\lim_{\alpha\rightarrow 0^+}h(\alpha)=1/2$. Similar result also holds for $\#\{n\leq x:P_y^+(n)<P_y^+(n+1)\}$. These improve Rivat's result (2001) and Wang's result (2019).

The entire setup can be seen as a Shimura curve analogue of Serre's letter to Tate on quaternions and modular forms.     💡 解决Browkin p进连分数中关于周期性的猜想，证明对任意t存在无穷多整数平方根具有周期长度2t。
    📖 中文摘要

    多位作者在p进数域中引入并研究了连分数，旨在发展经典实连分数理论的类似物。本文中，我们关注Browkin的连分数算法，其中若干基本问题仍未解决，特别是关于二次无理数的周期展开。本文解决了文献[6]中遗留的关于周期性的猜想。作为推论，我们证明对于每个正整数t，存在无穷多整数平方根，其Browkin连分数展开是周期的且周期长度为2t。这也解决了源于先前关于整数平方根可能周期长度的经典结果的问题。此外，我们给出了二次无理数周期性的新充分条件，并推导了相应周期长度的显式界。这些结果也限制了非周期Browkin连分数展开的二次无理数的可能行为，从而为研究Lagrange定理的类似物是否成立提供了新工具。最后，我们对二次无理数的Browkin展开进行了计算研究，特别关注整数平方根的行为和寻找可能的非周期例子。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Continued fractions have been introduced and studied in the field of $p$--adic numbers by several authors, with the aim of developing analogues of the classical theory of real continued fractions. In this paper, we focus on Browkin's continued fraction algorithm, for which several fundamental questions remain open, especially concerning periodic expansions of quadratic irrationals. In this paper, we solve a conjecture about periodicity left open in [6]. As a consequence, we prove that, for every positive integer $t$, there exist infinitely many square roots of integers whose Browkin continued fraction expansion is periodic with period length $2t$. This also settles a problem originating from previous classical results on the possible period lengths of square roots of integers. Moreover, we provide new sufficient conditions for the periodicity of quadratic irrationals and derive explicit bounds for the corresponding period lengths. These results also restrict the possible behaviour of a quadratic irrational whose Browkin continued fraction expansion is non-periodic, and therefore provide new tools for investigating whether an analogue of Lagrange's theorem can hold. Finally, we also provide a computational study of the Browkin expansions of quadratic irrationals, with particular attention to the behaviour of square roots of integers and to the search for possible non-periodic examples.

    💡 研究非缺项幂级数在Pisot或Salem数处特殊值的代数独立性，给出三个值代数独立的新判别法。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究幂级数f(β^{-1})特殊值的代数独立性，其中β为固定的Pisot或Salem数。特别地，我们考虑f(X)=∑_{n≥0} t(n) X^{w(n)}不是缺项级数且不假设满足任何特殊函数方程（如Mahler型函数方程）的情形。在我们的主要结果中，我们给出了三个值代数独立性的新判别法。应用主要结果，我们证明以下三个值代数独立：∑_{n=3}^∞ ⌊n^y⌋ β^{-⌊n^{log log n}⌋}, ∑_{n=3}^∞ β^{-⌊n^{log log n}⌋}, ∑_{n=1}^∞ β^{-⌊n^{log n}⌋}，其中y为任意正实数。由于我们的判别法灵活，我们在选择系数(t(n))_{n≥0}和指数(w(n))_{n≥0}时有很大自由度。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the algebraic independence of special values of power series $f(\beta^{-1})$, where $\beta$ is a fixed Pisot or Salem number. In particular, we consider the case where $f(X)=\sum_{n\geq 0} t(n) X^{w(n)}$ is not a lacunary series and is not assumed to satisfy any special functional equation, such as a Mahler-type functional equation. In our main results, we give a new criterion of the algebraic independence of three values. Applying our main results, we prove that the following three values are algebraically independent: \[ % \begin{gathered} \sum_{n=3}^{\infty}\lfloor n^{y}\rfloor\beta^{-\lfloor n^{\log \log n}\rfloor},\quad \sum_{n=3}^{\infty}\beta^{-\lfloor n^{\log \log n}\rfloor},\quad \sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\beta^{-\lfloor n^{\log n}\rfloor}, % \end{gathered} \] where $y$ is an arbitrary positive real number. Since our criterion is flexible, we have considerable freedom in choosing the coefficients $(t(n))_{n\geq 0}$ and the exponents $(w(n))_{n\geq 0}$.

    Long runs of integers with small prime factors and the divisor function of $n!$
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究n!的除数函数跳跃K(n)的下界。
    • 方法采用Erdős-Rankin构造的加权变体和平均论证。
    • 主要结果将Erdős-Graham-Ivić-Pomerance的界改进了log_3 n阶，达到Rankin素数间隙下界的阶。
    • 该工作揭示了小素因子长游程与素数间隙问题的深刻联系。
    • 改进的论证部分由生成式AI系统辅助形成。
    💡 改进Erdős-Graham-Ivić-Pomerance关于n!除数函数跳跃的界，达到与Rankin素数间隙下界相同的阶。
    📖 中文摘要

    设d为除数函数，K(n)为满足d((n+K)!) ≥ 2d(n!)的最小正整数K。Erdős、Graham、Ivić和Pomerance证明了对于无穷多个n，K(n) > (1/9)(log n)(log_2 n)(log_4 n)/(log_3 n)^3。我们通过一个log_3 n阶的因子改进了这一结果，使界达到与Rankin 1938年关于连续素数间隙下界相同的阶。这两个问题密切相关，但长无素数区间本身并不产生大的K(n)值：需要的是Erdős-Rankin构造的加权变体。我们遵循Erdős、Graham、Ivić和Pomerance的方法，用平均论证替换关键估计，允许某些整数未被覆盖。这一改进在与公开可用的生成式AI系统Claude Fable 5.1的私人交互中形成并证明；论证由作者验证并呈现于此。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $d$ be the divisor function, and let $K(n)$ be the least positive integer $K$ for which $d((n + K)!) \ge 2d(n!)$. Erd\H{o}s, Graham, Ivi\'c and Pomerance proved that, for infinitely many $n$, \begin{equation*} K(n) > (1/9)(\log n)(\log_{2} n)(\log_{4} n)/(\log_{3} n)^3. \end{equation*} We improve upon this by a factor of order $\log_{3} n$, which brings the bound to the same order as Rankin's 1938 lower bound for gaps between consecutive primes. The two problems are closely related, but a long prime-free interval does not by itself produce a large value of $K(n)$: what is needed is a weighted variant of the Erd\H{o}s--Rankin construction. We follow the method of Erd\H{o}s, Graham, Ivi\'c and Pomerance, replacing a key estimate by an averaging argument that permits some integers to remain uncovered. This improvement was formulated and proved during a private interaction with Claude Fable 5.1, a publicly available generative-AI system; the argument is verified and presented here by the author.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15597

    • 主要结果证明在2U⊕L型格上恰有27个这样的乘积，对应85个辅助代数。
    • 该分类与顶点算子超代数密切相关。

    💡 分类奇异权全纯自守乘积，证明在2U⊕L型格上恰有27个，并联系BKM超代数与顶点算子超代数。
    📖 中文摘要

    在证明月光猜想后，Borcherds于1995年提问：奇异权的全纯自守乘积是否只有有限多个？我们证明，在2U⊕L型格上，不计共轭恰有27个这样的乘积。分类通过将每个乘积在标准1维尖点处等同于一个BKM超代数的超分母来进行，该超代数带有仿射Lie超代数ĝ的 graded 模结构。辅助代数ĝ被证明满足强条件，将可能性缩减到初始的1372个候选。消除论证后恰好留下85个代数ĝ，对应27个乘积。该分类与某些特殊的顶点算子超代数族密切相关。我们的结果为将仿射Lie超代数扩展为具有模性的双曲型提供了系统框架，推广了Feingold和Frenkel (1983)的工作。

    🌐 English Abstract

    After proving the moonshine conjecture, Borcherds asked in 1995 whether there exist only finitely many holomorphic automorphic products of singular weight. We show that, up to conjugation, there are exactly 27 such products on lattices of the form $2U\oplus L$. The classification proceeds by identifying each product at a standard $1$-dimensional cusp with the superdenominator of a BKM superalgebra that carries a graded module structure over an affine Lie superalgebra $\hat{\mathfrak{g}}$. The auxiliary algebras $\hat{\mathfrak{g}}$ are shown to satisfy strong conditions that reduce the possibilities to an initial list of 1372 candidates. An elimination argument leaves exactly 85 algebras $\hat{\mathfrak{g}}$ that correspond to the 27 products. This classification is closely connected with certain distinguished families of vertex operator superalgebras. Our results provide a systematic framework for extending affine Lie superalgebras to hyperbolic ones with modularity, generalizing the work of Feingold and Frenkel (1983).

    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明特征零域上七次Hermite-Joubert问题：若F为特征零域，E/F为七次域扩张，则E由元素a生成，满足tr_{E/F}(a)=tr_{E/F}(a^3)=0，即极小多项式形如λ^7+c_2λ^5+c_4λ^3+c_5λ^2+c_6λ+c_7，其中c_i∈F。这由显式公式给出：二元七次形式的系数次数为七、阶为五的协变量，在生成元θ处取值并除以其极小多项式在θ处的导数。我们还宣布一般定理，将在准备中的姊妹篇中证明：在任意无限域、任意特征上，每个七次étale代数包含本原元a满足c_1(a)=c_3(a)=0。此外，任意域的每个七次域扩张都有生成元a满足c_1(a)=c_3(a)=0。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove the degree-seven case of the Hermite--Joubert problem in characteristic zero: if $F$ is a field of characteristic zero and $E/F$ is a field extension of degree seven, then $E$ is generated by an element $a$ with $\text{tr}_{E/F}(a)=\text{tr}_{E/F}(a^{3})=0$, that is, with minimal polynomial of the form $\lambda^{7}+c_{2}\lambda^{5}+c_{4}\lambda^{3}+c_{5}\lambda^{2}+c_{6}\lambda+c_{7}$, where the $c_i$'s belong to $F$. This is given by an explicit formula: a covariant of the binary septic of coefficient degree seven and order five, evaluated at a generator $\theta$ and divided by the derivative of its minimal polynomial evaluated at $\theta$. We also announce the general theorem, which will be proved in a companion paper in preparation: over every infinite field, in every characteristic, every \'etale algebra of degree seven contains a primitive element $a$ with $c_{1}(a)=c_{3}(a)=0$. Moreover, every field extension of degree seven of an arbitrary field has a generator $a$ with $c_{1}(a)=c_{3}(a)=0$.

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Riemann zeta函数零点纵坐标的Stieltjes变换的Cauchy极限。

    • 在有限域上，得到了zeta函数对数导数的精确Cauchy律和显式误差。
    • 该工作将Cauchy极限现象从数域推广到有限域上的簇。
    💡 证明Riemann zeta零点投影纵坐标的对称截断Stieltjes变换的无条件Cauchy极限，并给出有限域上zeta函数的Cauchy律。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明了Riemann zeta函数零点投影纵坐标的对称截断Stieltjes变换的无条件Cauchy极限。临界线上ζ的对数导数的适当归一化具有相同的极限，只要位于临界线右侧的零点（计重数至高度2T）到临界线的距离之和为o(T)；我们给出了比较误差的显式界。证明依赖于正平稳点测度的Stieltjes变换的收敛定理，其计数偏差满足可积性准则。该定理通过比较周期点测度的变换，结合截断界和转移定理得到。在有限域上，经典的余切恒等式给出簇的zeta函数对数导数的Cauchy极限：对于非平凡纯上同调因子，律恰好是Cauchy的，而Poincaré对偶给出完整zeta函数的显式误差，包括对递增次数的光滑超曲面的Cauchy极限，且关于基域一致。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove an unconditional Cauchy limit for a symmetrically truncated Stieltjes transform of the projected ordinates of the zeros of the Riemann zeta function. A suitable normalization of the logarithmic derivative of $\zeta$ on the critical line has the same limit provided that the sum of the distances to the critical line of the zeros lying to its right, counted with multiplicity up to height $2T$, is $o(T)$; we give an explicit bound on the comparison error. The proof rests on a convergence theorem for the Stieltjes transform of positive stationary point measures whose counting discrepancy satisfies an integrability criterion. This theorem is obtained by comparison with the transforms of periodic point measures, combined with truncation bounds and a transfer theorem. Over finite fields, a classical cotangent identity yields Cauchy limits for the logarithmic derivatives of zeta functions of varieties: the law is exactly Cauchy for nonconstant pure cohomological factors, and Poincar\'e duality gives explicit errors for full zeta functions, including Cauchy limits for smooth hypersurfaces of increasing degree, uniformly in the base field.

    Subset-Sum Density Realization in Locally Finite Abelian Groups
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究局部有限阿贝尔群中子集和密度的极限点集可实现性。
    • 通过滤过和有限子群构造子集，分析其子集和密度的极限行为。
    • 证明 $|2G|=\infty$ 时任意区间可实现，并给出正密度下的无限子集和封闭性。
    • 结果推广了子集和密度理论，揭示了群结构与密度极限的关系。
    💡 证明在局部有限阿贝尔群中，子集和密度极限点集可实现为任意区间，并给出正密度结果。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 $G$ 是可数局部有限阿贝尔群，且 $G_1\leq G_2\leq\cdots$，$\bigcup_{i\geq1}G_i=G$ 是 $G$ 的有限子群滤过。对 $A\subseteq G$，令 $\mathcal P(A)$ 表示 $A$ 中元素的所有有限子集和，$2G:=\{2g:g\in G\}$。我们证明 $|2G|=\infty$ 当且仅当对每个滤过和每个区间 $[\alpha,\beta]\subseteq[0,1]$，存在 $A\subseteq G$ 使得序列 $(|\mathcal P(A)\cap G_i|/|G_i|)_{i\geq1}$ 的极限点集恰好是 $[\alpha,\beta]$。我们还证明了一个不需要 $|2G|=\infty$ 的正密度结果：若 $G$ 无限且 $\mathcal P(A)$ 沿给定滤过有正上密度，则存在无限集 $B\subseteq\mathcal P(A)$ 使得 $B+B\subseteq\mathcal P(A)$。结合实现定理，我们证明当 $|2G|=\infty$ 时，每个满足 $0\leq\alpha\leq\beta\leq1$ 且 $\beta>0$ 的区间 $[\alpha,\beta]$ 都可由具有此附加性质的集合 $A$ 实现。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $G$ be a countable locally finite abelian group, and let \[ G_1\leq G_2\leq\cdots, \qquad \bigcup_{i\geq1}G_i=G, \] be any filtration of $G$ by finite subgroups. For $A\subseteq G$, let $\mathcal P(A)$ denote the set of all finite subset sums of elements of $A$, and let $2G:=\{2g:g\in G\}$. We prove that $|2G|=\infty$ if and only if for every filtration and every interval $[\alpha,\beta]\subseteq[0,1]$, there exists $A\subseteq G$ such that the set of limit points of \[ \left( \frac{|\mathcal P(A)\cap G_i|}{|G_i|} \right)_{i\geq1} \] is exactly $[\alpha,\beta]$. We also prove a positive-density result that does not require $|2G|=\infty$: if $G$ is infinite and $\mathcal P(A)$ has positive upper density along the given filtration, then there is an infinite set $B\subseteq \mathcal P(A)$ such that $B+B\subseteq \mathcal P(A)$. Combining this with the realization theorem, we show that, when $|2G|=\infty$, every interval $[\alpha,\beta]$ with $0\leq\alpha\leq\beta\leq1$ and $\beta>0$ can be realized by a set $A$ with this additional property.

    • 结果推广了 Bogomolov 维数定理，并应用于高度间隙和 Pell 方程。
    💡 证明支配子簇的纤维挠点 Zariski 稠密当且仅当混合 Betti 秩最大，并给出应用。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 $\mathcal G\to S$ 是光滑复代数基上的半阿贝尔概型，阿贝尔相对维数为 $g$，环面秩为 $r$。我们证明：一个支配不可约子簇若不包含在真相对特殊子簇中，则其纤维挠点 Zariski 稠密当且仅当其混合 Betti 秩为 $2(g+r)$。特别地，其维数至少为 $g+r$。对每个素数，主挠点即使在最大秩点中也稠密。例外轨迹包括广义 Ribet 轨迹。在代数数上，我们证明一般小点迫使完全混合秩，只要阿贝尔 Betti 秩最大。这给出了从阿贝尔相对 Bogomolov 维数定理到任意环面扩张的转移，以及阿贝尔挠上的环面高度间隙和节点 Pell 方程的阶线性粘合高度界。证明结合了标记 $1$-motives 的代数商、二次 nef 补偿、中心单值计算和混合 Betti 层的代数性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\mathcal G\to S$ be a semiabelian scheme over a smooth complex algebraic base, with abelian relative dimension $g$ and toric rank $r$. We prove that a dominating irreducible subvariety not contained in a proper relative special subvariety has Zariski-dense fibrewise torsion if and only if its mixed Betti rank is $2(g+r)$. In particular, its dimension is at least $g+r$. For every prime, primary torsion is then dense even among points of maximal rank. The exceptional loci include generalized Ribet loci. Over algebraic numbers, we prove that generic small points force full mixed rank whenever the abelian Betti rank is maximal. This yields a transfer from abelian relative Bogomolov dimension theorems to arbitrary toric extensions, together with toric height gaps above abelian torsion and order-linear gluing-height bounds for nodal Pell equations. The proof combines algebraic quotients of marked $1$-motives, quadratic nef compensation, a central-monodromy calculation, and algebraicity of mixed Betti strata.

    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究 $[n]:=\{1,2,\ldots,n\}$ 的稀疏随机着色中具有指定颜色模式的算术级数的阈值和涨落，其中每个元素独立地根据给定概率向量着色。对于任何容许的有序调色板，我们确定彩色算术级数出现的完整多参数阈值区域。阈值由两个竞争机制控制：全局一阶矩条件和局部颜色可用性条件，导致多面体可满足区域和分段多面体阈值曲面。在可满足区域内，我们建立给定长度彩色算术级数数量的渐近正态性，并给出 Wasserstein 距离的显式收敛速率。在阈值曲面上，我们识别出三种不同的渐近区域：泊松、带混合泊松跳的复合泊松和带均匀跳的复合泊松，经过适当归一化。这些结果在统一框架中完整描述了稀疏随机着色下一般彩色算术级数的阈值和涨落行为，插值于二项随机子集中的经典无色/单色级数和多色（包括彩虹）算术级数之间。

For any admissible ordered palette of colors, we determine the full multi-parameter threshold region for the appearance of a colorful arithmetic progression. The threshold is governed by two competing mechanisms: a global first-moment condition and a local color-availability condition, resulting in a polyhedral satisfiability region, with a piecewise-polyhedral threshold surface. In the satisfiability region we establish asymptotic normality for the number of colorful arithmetic progressions of a given length, with an explicit rate of convergence in Wasserstein distance. On the threshold surface, we identify three distinct asymptotic regimes: Poisson, compound Poisson with mixed Poisson jumps, and compound Poisson with uniform jumps, after an appropriate normalization.     We show that for any henselian valued field of mixed characteristic $(0,p)$, the (existential) $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{val}}$-theory of the valued field is determined by the (existential) theory of the value group in $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{oag}}$ with a constant for $v(p)$ and the (existential) theory of the residue ring $\mathcal{O}_v/(p)$ in an expansion $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{Witt}}$ of the language of rings, provided $\mathcal{O}_v/(p)$ is semi perfect.     📖 中文摘要

    经典 Schwarz 导数可直接从二阶线性方程的系数计算。我们将这种系数观点推广到有理数上单位结合微分代数上的任意阶首一算子。归一化系数自由生成协变微分下的通用规范协变量。标量情形下的射影归一化，随后对称化，给出高阶 Schwarz 导数，其在标架和参数变化下具有相容的有限定律。在复曲线上，这些不变量连同系数联络和二次数据重构算子。在亏格 $g\geq2$ 的紧曲线上，典范 Szeg\H{o} 核将构造转化为恢复丛几何的工具。三次不变量的括号恢复具有不同三次项的无环线丛的标记扩张。通过 $4g-1$ 次的高阶不变量分离无环线丛并检测其自扩张。对任意秩的无环丛，一点处的完整不变量射流生成典范单值性的结合张成。相同的通用系数演算给出 Siegel 模簇上的自守张量。对亏格二过滤辛联络，若 Kodaira--Spencer 映射可逆，矩阵 Schwarz 不变量确定 Hodge 滤过选择的标量方程获得表观奇点的位置。精确 Picard--Fuchs 计算展示这些除子并用 Siegel 模形式表达其方程。精确计算在可重复的辅助包中提供。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The classical Schwarzian can be computed directly from the coefficients of a second-order linear equation. We extend this coefficient viewpoint to monic operators of arbitrary order over unital associative differential algebras over the rationals. The normalized coefficients freely generate the universal gauge covariants under covariant differentiation. Projective normalization in the scalar case, followed by symmetrization, gives higher Schwarzians with compatible finite laws under changes of frame and parameter. On a complex curve, these invariants, together with the coefficient connection and quadratic datum, reconstruct the operator. On a compact curve of genus $g\ge2$, canonical Szeg\H{o} kernels turn the construction into a tool for recovering bundle geometry. A bracket of the cubic invariant recovers marked extensions of acyclic line bundles with distinct cubics. Higher invariants through degree $4g-1$ separate acyclic line bundles and detect their self-extensions. For acyclic bundles of arbitrary rank, the full invariant jets at a point generate the associative span of the canonical monodromy. For genus-two filtered symplectic connections with invertible Kodaira--Spencer map, matrix Schwarzian invariants determine where scalar equations selected by the Hodge filtration acquire apparent singularities.   73. [2604.23097] 线性化多项式码的壳
    On the hull of linearized polynomial codes
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究线性化多项式码的壳维数。
    • 通过 Gram 矩阵方法，给出像码和秩距离码的壳-秩公式。
    • 分类 LCD 轨迹并计算 LCD 密度趋于 1。
    • 结果为纠缠辅助量子纠错码提供了理论工具。
    💡 研究两类线性码的壳维数，给出主壳-秩公式和 LCD 密度结果。
    📖 中文摘要

    受纠缠辅助量子纠错码启发，其中壳维数决定所需预共享纠缠对的数量，我们研究由 $\mathbb{F}_{q^m}$ 上 $q$-多项式算子定义的两类 $\mathbb{F}_q$-线性码的壳。我们的主要工具是统一的 Gram 矩阵方法。对于像码 $\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{\alpha})=\operatorname{im}\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\alpha}}$，其中 $\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\alpha}}=\sum_i\alpha_iF_i$，我们证明主壳-秩公式 $\dim\operatorname{Hull}(\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{\alpha}))=\operatorname{rank}(\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\alpha}})-\operatorname{rank}(G(\boldsymbol{\alpha}))$，其中 $G(\boldsymbol{\alpha})$ 是 $\mathbb{F}_q$ 上的关联 Gram 矩阵。特化到 $C_{\lambda,\mu}=\operatorname{im}(\lambda x+\mu L(x))$，我们得到二次 Gram 铅笔 $\lambda^2G_0+\lambda\mu G_1+\mu^2G_2$，其行列式描述 $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{F}_q)$ 中的 LCD 轨迹。我们还处理 $\mathbb{F}_{q^m}$-线性秩距离码 $\mathcal{C}=\langle X,F_1,\ldots,F_k\rangle_{\mathbb{F}_{q^m}}$ 与 Delsarte 内积，其中 $k\times k$ Gram 矩阵决定壳维数。对于 $L(X)=X^{q^k}$，$d=\gcd(k,m)$，所得循环 Gram 矩阵给出闭式判别式，并在 $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{F}_{q^m})$ 上的四种双射配置中的三种给出完全分类。在剩余情形，壳维数等于 $\delta=\dim_{\mathbb{F}_q}(\operatorname{im}\phi_{\lambda,\mu}\cap\ker\phi_{\lambda,\mu}^{\dagger})$，极端条件 $\delta=d$ 由显式迹各向同性准则刻画。我们最后给出 LCD 和非 LCD 点的精确计数，表明 LCD 密度当 $q\to\infty$ 时趋于 $1$，并给出 $\mathbb{F}_{64}$ 上的例子和 SageMath 验证。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Motivated by entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting codes, where the hull dimension determines the number of required pre-shared entangled pairs, we study hulls of two families of $\mathbb{F}_q$-linear codes defined by $q$-polynomial operators over $\mathbb{F}_{q^m}$. Our main tool is a unified Gram-matrix method. For image codes $\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{\alpha})=\operatorname{im}\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\alpha}}$, with $\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\alpha}}=\sum_i\alpha_iF_i$, we prove the master hull--rank formula $\dim\operatorname{Hull}(\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{\alpha}))=\operatorname{rank}(\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\alpha}})-\operatorname{rank}(G(\boldsymbol{\alpha}))$, where $G(\boldsymbol{\alpha})$ is the associated Gram matrix over $\mathbb{F}_q$. Specializing to $C_{\lambda,\mu}=\operatorname{im}(\lambda x+\mu L(x))$, we obtain a quadratic Gram pencil $\lambda^2G_0+\lambda\mu G_1+\mu^2G_2$ whose determinant describes the LCD locus in $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{F}_q)$. We also treat $\mathbb{F}_{q^m}$-linear rank-distance codes $\mathcal{C}=\langle X,F_1,\ldots,F_k\rangle_{\mathbb{F}_{q^m}}$ with the Delsarte inner product, where a $k\times k$ Gram matrix over $\mathbb{F}_{q^m}$ determines the hull dimension. For $L(X)=X^{q^k}$, with $d=\gcd(k,m)$, the resulting circulant Gram matrices yield a closed-form discriminant and a complete classification in three of the four bijectivity configurations over $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{F}_{q^m})$. In the remaining case, the hull dimension equals $\delta=\dim_{\mathbb{F}_q}(\operatorname{im}\phi_{\lambda,\mu}\cap\ker\phi_{\lambda,\mu}^{\dagger})$, and the extremal condition $\delta=d$ is characterized by an explicit trace-isotropy criterion. We conclude with an exact count of LCD and non-LCD points, showing that the LCD density tends to $1$ as $q\to\infty$, together with a worked example over $\mathbb{F}_{64}$ and a SageMath verification.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23097

    我们引入一类显式的 tempered Laurent 多项式（Villegas 和 Doran--Kerr 意义下），变量数 $n\leq4$，包括所有反典范类非常丰沛的光滑 Fano 三维簇的 Landau--Ginzburg 模型。我们验证它包含各种 Fano 四维簇的 Landau--Ginzburg 模型，这些四维簇是光滑环簇和平面 Grassmann 簇中的完全交，或是箭图旗零轨迹。我们讨论对算术镜像对称猜想的启示，这是 Golyshev--Kerr--Sasaki 提出的研究 Fano 簇 Apéry 常数的 Hodge 理论方法。利用 Kerr 证明的算术镜像对称猜想的部分情形，我们提供三个特定代数类之间镜像对称对应的例子。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce an explicit class of tempered Laurent polynomials in the sense of Villegas and Doran--Kerr in $n \leqslant 4$ variables including all Landau--Ginzburg models for smooth Fano threefolds with very ample anticanonical class. We check that it contains Landau--Ginzburg models for various Fano fourfolds which are complete intersections in smooth toric varieties and Grassmannians of planes, or are quiver flag zero loci. We discuss implications to Arithmetic Mirror Symmetry conjecture, a Hodge-theoretic approach to the study of Ap\'{e}ry constants of Fano varieties proposed by Golyshev--Kerr--Sasaki. Using the partial case of Arithmetic Mirror Symmetry conjecture proved by Kerr, we provide three examples of a Mirror Symmetry correspondence between specific algebraic classes.

  75. [2605.29947] 曲线 $p$-进 Simpson 对应中的提升无关性问题
    Lift-independence problem in the $p$-adic Simpson correspondence for curves
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 $p$-进 Simpson 对应中 Higgs 丛对提升的依赖性。
    • 定义提升无关 Higgs 丛，并证明低秩时幂零或 Higgs 场为零。
    • 构造反例表明半稳定提升无关丛可具有非零 Higgs 场。
    • 结果揭示了提升选择对 Simpson 对应的非平凡影响。
    💡 研究 $p$-进 Simpson 对应中 Higgs 丛对提升的依赖性，证明提升无关 Higgs 丛的性质。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 $X$ 是完备代数闭 $p$-进域 $\mathbf C$ 上的真光滑刚性解析簇。固定 $\exp$ 的连续延拓 $\operatorname{Exp}$。Faltings 在曲线情形和 Heuer 证明：$X$ 在 $B_{\mathrm{dR}}^+/t^2$ 上的任何提升 $\widetilde X$ 诱导 $X_{\text{ét}}$ 上 Higgs 丛范畴与 $X_v$ 上 $v$-向量丛范畴之间的等价。改变 $\widetilde X$ 会非平凡地改变对应。这引出自然问题：固定 $v$-向量丛关联的 Higgs 丛的同构类如何随 $\widetilde X$ 变化，此问题尚未被系统研究。本文中，我们处理 $X$ 是亏格 $g\geq2$ 曲线的情形。更精确地，若一个 Higgs 丛对每个提升 $\widetilde X$ 在 $p$-进 Simpson 对应下对应相同的 $v$-丛，则称其为提升无关的。我们证明以下结果：(1) 每个秩 $r\leq g$ 的提升无关 Higgs 丛是幂零的。(2) 每个秩 $r\leq\sqrt{g}+1$ 的半稳定提升无关 Higgs 丛有零 Higgs 场。(3) 相反，即使 Faltings 猜想选出的类中，消失性也失败：总存在一个度为 $0$ 的提升无关半稳定 Higgs 丛具有非零 Higgs 场。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $X$ be a proper smooth rigid analytic variety over a complete algebraically closed $p$-adic field $\mathbf C$. Fix a continuation $\operatorname{Exp}$ of $\exp$. Faltings, in the curve case, and Heuer showed that any lifting $\widetilde X$ of $X$ over $B_{\mathrm{dR}}^+/t^2$ induces an equivalence between the category of Higgs bundles on $X_{\text{\'et}}$ and the category of $v$-vector bundles on $X_v$. Changing $\widetilde X$ is known to alter the correspondence nontrivially. This raises the natural question of how the isomorphism class of the Higgs bundle associated with a fixed $v$-vector bundle varies with $\widetilde X$, a question that has not been systematically studied. In this paper, we address this question when $X$ is a curve of genus $g\ge 2$. More precisely, we call a Higgs bundle lift-independent if it corresponds to the same $v$-bundle under the $p$-adic Simpson correspondence for every lifting $\widetilde X$. We prove the following results. (1) Every lift-independent Higgs bundle of rank $r\le g$ is nilpotent. (2) Every semistable lift-independent Higgs bundle of rank $r\le \sqrt{g}+1$ has zero Higgs field. (3) In contrast, vanishing fails even within the class singled out by Faltings' conjecture: there always exists a lift-independent semistable Higgs bundle of degree $0$ with nonzero Higgs field.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29947
  76. [2606.02870] 极大 Dynkin 条纹
    On maximal Dynkin friezes
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 $B_n$ 和 $D_n$ 型 Dynkin 条纹的极大元。
    • 通过构造仿射簇变量上的大正整数点，显式给出条纹。
    • 猜想所得最大元是相应型的最大可能值。
    • 结果补全了有限 Dynkin 型极大条纹的分类。
    💡 构造 $B_n$ 和 $D_n$ 型极大正整数值条纹，并猜想其最大性。
    📖 中文摘要

    正整数上的 Dynkin 条纹的极大元最近已对所有有限 Dynkin 型确定，除了 $B_n$ 和 $D_n$。在本注记中，我们显式构造 $B_n$ 型（分别 $D_n$ 型）仿射簇变量上的大正整数点，给出 $B_n$ 型（分别 $D_n$ 型）正整数上的条纹，其最大元为 $F_{n+1}F_{n+2}-1$（分别 $F_nF_{n+1}-1$），其中 $F_k$ 是第 $k$ 个 Fibonacci 数。我们猜想这些是各自 Dynkin 型的最大可能元。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The maximal entries of Dynkin friezes over the positive integers have recently been determined for all finite Dynkin types except $B_n$ and $D_n$. In this note, we explicitly construct large positive integral points on affine cluster varieties of type $B_n$ (resp. $D_n$), giving rise to friezes of types $B_n$ (resp. $D_n$) over the positive integers with largest entries $F_{n+1} F_{n+2} - 1$ (resp. $F_n F_{n+1} - 1$) where $F_k$ is the $k$-th Fibonacci number. We conjecture that these are the maximal possible entries for their respective Dynkin types.

  77. [2608.19507] 通过对角 GP-图研究对角方程组解数：一般与 Hermitian 形式情形
    On the number of solutions of systems of diagonal equations through diagonal GP-graphs: the general and the Hermitian-form cases
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究对角方程组解数的计算。
    • 引入对角 GP-图，用游走、邻接矩阵和谱给出解数表达式。
    • 对 Hermitian 形式情形，导出组合公式和递推关系。
    • 结果为有限域上对角方程组的解数提供了图论方法。
    💡 引入对角 GP-图，给出对角方程组解数的表达式和 Hermitian 情形的组合公式。
    📖 中文摘要

    对任意 $m,s\in\mathbb{N}$，我们研究单项对角方程组 $X_1^{k_i}+\cdots+X_s^{k_i}=\beta_i$（$1\leq i\leq m$）在 $(\mathbb{F}_q)^s$ 中的解数 $N_{m\times s,q}(\kappa,\beta)$，其中 $\kappa=(k_1,\ldots,k_m)\in\mathbb{N}^m$，$\beta=(\beta_1,\ldots,\beta_m)\in(\mathbb{F}_q)^m$。我们证明该数可通过对角 GP-图 $\Gamma(\kappa,q)$ 的某些数据获得。这是一类新图：$\Gamma(\kappa,q)$ 是顶点集为有限域 $\mathbb{F}_q$ 的有向图，从 $u$ 到 $v$ 有弧当且仅当 $v-u\in R_{\kappa}=\{(x^{k_1},\ldots,x^{k_m}):x\in\mathbb{F}_q^*\}$。特别地，我们给出 $N_{m\times s,q}(\kappa,\beta)$ 的三种不同表达式：一种用游走，一种用 $\Gamma(\kappa,q)$ 的邻接矩阵，最后一种用 $\Gamma(\kappa,q)$ 的谱。最后，我们通过已知的 Hermitian 形式图谱（可视为对角 GP-图）显式推导出单项齐次对角方程组 $X_1^{q^{\ell_i}+1}+\cdots+X_s^{q^{\ell_i}+1}=0$（$1\leq i\leq m$）的解数 $N_m(s,q)=N_{m\times s,q}(\kappa_\ell,0)$ 的组合公式，其中 $\kappa_\ell=(\ell_1,\ldots,\ell_m)=(1,3,\ldots,2m-1)$，$m\geq2$。对任意 $m,s\in\mathbb{N}$，我们给出 $N_m(s,q)\in\mathbb{Z}[q]$ 的一般求和和递推公式。对小情形 $N_1(s,q)$、$N_2(s,q)$ 和 $N_m(s,q)$（$1\leq s\leq5$），我们给出显式表达式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For any $m, s \in \mathbb{N}$, we study the number $N_{m\times s,q}(\kappa, \beta)$ of solutions $(x_1,\ldots,x_s) \in (\mathbb{F}_q)^s$ of the monic system of diagonal equations $$ X_{1}^{k_i} + \cdots + X_{s}^{k_i}= \beta_i, \qquad (1\le i \le m), $$ with $\kappa=(k_1,\ldots,k_m) \in \mathbb{N}^m$ and $\beta=(\beta_1,\ldots,\beta_m) \in (\mathbb{F}_q)^m$. We show that this number can be obtained in terms of some data of \textit{diagonal} GP-graphs $\Gamma(\kappa,q)$. This is a new family of graphs that we introduce here, $\Gamma(\kappa,q)$, with $\kappa = (k_1,\ldots,k_m) \in \mathbb{N}^{m}$, is the directed graph with vertex set the finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$ and there is an arc from $u$ to $v$ if and only if $v-u \in R_{\kappa} = \{ (x^{k_1},\ldots,x^{k_m}) : x \in \mathbb{F}_{q}^*\}$. In particular, we give three different expressions for $N_{m\times s,q}(\kappa, \beta)$: one in terms of walks, another in terms of adjacency matrices of $\Gamma(\kappa,q)$ and the last one in terms of the spectrum of $\Gamma(\kappa,q)$. Finally, we explicitly derive combinatorial formulas for the number of solutions $N_{m}(s,q) = N_{m\times s,q}(\kappa_\ell, 0)$ of monic homogeneous systems of diagonal equations of the form $$ X_1^{q^{\ell_i}+1} + \cdots + X_s^{q^{\ell_i}+1} = 0 \qquad (1\le i \le m),$$ with $\kappa_\ell=(\ell_1,\ldots,\ell_m)=(1,3,\ldots,2m-1)$ and $m\ge 2$, via the known spectrum of Hermitian-form graphs, which can be viewed as diagonal GP-graphs. For any $m,s \in \mathbb{N}$, we give general summation and recursive formulas for $N_m(s,q) \in \mathbb{Z}[q]$. For the small cases $N_{1}(s,q)$, $N_{2}(s,q)$ and $N_{m}(s,q)$, with $1\le s \le 5$, we give explicit expressions.
